# Adversarial test matrix

Every production security/scheduling invariant enforced by Kiwi has an
identifier here. CI runs the adversarial lane (`make test-adversarial`) over
these tests; an invariant with no listed test is treated as a gap.

| ID | Invariant | Threat | Implementation | Test file | Test function | Expected |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SEC-ENV-001 | Distributed jobs never inherit the runner's host environment; only a fixed clean env plus declared vars | Host credential leak through `os.Environ()` | `internal/executor/executor.go` (`cleanExecutionEnv`, `mergeEnvMap`) | `internal/executor/env_security_test.go` | `TestCleanEnvNoHostSecrets` | sentinel host var absent; PATH/CI/KIWI present |
| SEC-ENV-002 | Host env inheritance is explicit opt-in only | Accidental opt-out | `executor.Options.InheritEnv` | `internal/executor/env_security_test.go` | `TestInheritEnvOptIn` | sentinel present only with InheritEnv |
| SEC-NET-001 | `network: none` cannot be regained via services; service networks are `--internal` | Services-network egress bypass | `internal/executor/services.go` | `internal/executor/backend_security_test.go` (serviceNetworkArgs) | `TestServiceNetworkInternalFlag` | `--internal` present when isolated |
| SEC-NET-002 | Service containers run hardened (`--cap-drop=ALL`, no-new-privileges, read-only, pids/memory/cpus, non-root) | Privileged service container | `internal/executor/services.go` | `internal/executor/backend_security_test.go` | service arg assertions | hardened flags always applied |
| SEC-NET-003 | Tart jobs refuse to run when isolation is required but unsupported | Tart egress bypass | `internal/executor/tart.go` | `internal/executor/backend_security_test.go` | `TestTartNetworkRefused` | fail-closed error before tart invocation |
| SEC-IMG-001 | Immutable image digests mandatory for untrusted jobs (`@sha256:`) | Mutable-tag supply chain | `executor.Options.RequireImmutableImages` | `internal/executor/executor_test.go` | `TestImmutableImageRejected` | tag-only image rejected |
| SEC-IMG-002 | Same for Tart VM references | `:latest` VM | `internal/executor/tart.go` | `internal/executor/backend_security_test.go` | `TestTartImmutableRefRejected` | rejected |
| SEC-SSH-001 | Tart SSH host authentication is never disabled | MITM via `StrictHostKeyChecking=no` | `internal/executor/tart.go` (per-job known_hosts, accept-new, IdentitiesOnly) | `internal/executor/backend_security_test.go` | `TestTartKeyGeneration` | per-job keypair + known_hosts |
| SEC-OUT-001 | Step output files are never host-read; reads go through the sandbox | Symlink `KIWI_OUTPUT` → host file | `executor` (`Backend.ReadFile`) | `internal/executor/outputs_security_test.go` | `TestStepOutputSymlinkRejected` | symlink output rejected |
| SEC-OUT-002 | Output reads bounded (`maxBytes`) | Output-file DoS | `Backend.ReadFile` cap | `internal/executor/outputs_security_test.go` | `TestOutputFileTooLarge` | step outputs error |
| SEC-STREAM-001 | >1 MiB log lines cannot deadlock execution | Pipe-fill deadlock | `internal/executor/streamlines.go` | `internal/executor/streamlines_test.go` | `TestStreamLinesLongLineNoDeadlock` | all bytes drained, truncation marker |
| SEC-LEASE-001 | Lease tokens never persist in plaintext | state.json theft → live capability | `model.Job.LeaseTokenHash` + server lease key | `internal/server/security_test.go` | `TestLeaseSurvivesRestart`, snapshot shape assertions | only HMAC stored |
| SEC-LEASE-002 | Cancelled/completed jobs cannot use their old lease | Dead-lease capability | `validActiveLease` | `internal/server/security_test.go` | `TestCancelledJobLeaseRejected` | 409 on blob/test/log/secret after cancel |
| SEC-LEASE-003 | Completion idempotency uses receipts, not leases | Duplicate completion | `completion_receipts` | `internal/server/security_test.go` | `TestCompletionReceiptIdempotency` | replay → 204/no mutation |
| SEC-LEASE-004 | Stale generations rejected | Generation replay | `validActiveLease` | `internal/server/security_test.go` | `TestStaleGenerationRejected` | 409 |
| SEC-LEASE-005 | Restart preserves valid leases | Duplicate deployment (restart requeue) | `recoverLeasesLocked` (no startup expiry) | `internal/server/security_test.go` | `TestLeaseSurvivesRestart` | job stays running/leased |
| SEC-LEASE-006 | Expired leases requeue exactly once | Lost runner | `recoverLeasesLocked` + retry budget | `internal/server/security_test.go` | `TestExpiredLeaseRequeued` | requeue, attempts++, budget respected |
| SEC-LEASE-007 | Runner self-cancels at acknowledged deadline | Network partition double-run | `internal/runner/runner.go` heartbeat deadline | `internal/runner/runner_test.go` | `TestHeartbeatTick` | cancel within 2s of deadline |
| SEC-CLONE-001 | Git credentials never sent to arbitrary hosts | Credential exfiltration | `gitEnvForRepo` host allowlist | `internal/runner/runner_test.go` | `TestGitEnvForRepo` | http/file/git/userinfo/query rejected |
| SEC-CLONE-002 | Credential config is host-scoped (`http.https://host/.extraHeader`) | Global header leak across remotes | `gitEnvForRepo` | `internal/runner/runner_test.go` | `TestGitEnvForRepo` | scoped config key emitted |
| SEC-CLONE-003 | Runner rejects non-loopback plaintext server | Credential interception | `validateServerURL` | `internal/runner/mtls_test.go` | `TestValidateServerURLNonLoopbackHTTPRejected` | error |

| FS-TAR-003 | Pre-existing symlink parents cannot redirect writes; nested destinations are opened through `safefs.OpenRootBeneath` (per-component no-follow walk that holds the final handle, closing the check/open TOCTOU window) | Symlink-parent escape | `internal/safefs/archive.go` (`OpenRootBeneath`, `cleanBeneath`), `internal/safefs/extract_unix.go` (`walkDirChain`, `openBeneathHandle`, `openParentChain`) | `internal/safefs/archive_test.go`, `internal/safefs/beneath_test.go` | `TestExtractRejectsSymlinkParent`, `TestOpenRootBeneathRejectsSymlinkedAncestor`, `TestOpenRootBeneathRejectsSymlinkedFinal`, `TestOpenRootBeneathTOCTOUBarrier` | error, target untouched; the held handle never escapes the root |
| FS-TAR-004 | Duplicate entries + case-fold collisions rejected | Overwrite smuggling | `internal/safefs` | `internal/safefs/archive_test.go` | `TestExtractRejectsDuplicateEntry`, `TestExtractRejectsCaseFoldCollision` | error |
| FS-TAR-005 | Compression bombs, depth, entry, file-size limits enforced | Zip bombs | `ExtractLimits` | `internal/safefs/archive_test.go` | `TestExtractRejectsCompressionBomb`, `TestExtractEnforcesDepthLimit`, `TestExtractEnforcesEntryLimit`, `TestExtractEnforcesFileLimit` | error |
| FS-TAR-006 | Cache restore goes through safefs and rejects a symlinked parent | Cache symlink-parent escape | `internal/cache` (`openExtractRoot`) | `internal/cache/manifest_test.go`, `internal/cache/durability_test.go` | `TestRestoreRejectsSymlinkParent`, `TestRestoreRejectsSymlinkedParent` | restore fails, outside untouched |
| SEC-CACHE-001 | Cache manifests signed, tamper-evident | Manifest poisoning | `internal/cache/manifest.go` | `internal/cache/manifest_test.go` | `TestManifestTamperRejected` | verify fails |

| SEC-LOG-013 | The runner journal ack watermark never advances past a failed or unacknowledged batch: the sender stops on permanent failure, the watermark covers a contiguous acked prefix, a legacy format-1 watermark replays the failed record, and amortized flushes are crash-safe | A failed batch permanently skipped by a later acked watermark (lost lines on restart) | sender/journal ack watermark + amortized flush (`internal/runner/logsink.go`, `logjournal.go`) | `internal/runner/logjournal_test.go` | `TestLogJournalLegacyWatermarkGapReplaysFailedBatch`, `TestLogSinkStopsOnSendFailureBeforeAckingPast`, `TestLogJournalAmortizedAckFlushBurst` | failed batch stays pending and is surfaced (Err/Remaining), no later batch sent; restart replays it exactly once with no sequence regression; bursts write fewer watermark flushes than acks while records above the durable watermark replay |

| SEC-RECOVERY-001 | DB runner revocation is ONE transaction: job requeue/cancel, lease clearing, runner `active_jobs`/counters, running/queued quota, dependents, run aggregation, and audit all commit together | Crash mid-kill-switch strands a runner slot, a quota reservation, or a live lease | `storage.RevokeRunnerLeases` (`internal/storage/postgres_recovery.go:48`); `RunnerDisableStore.DisableRunnerAndRevokeCert` (`internal/storage/postgres_disable.go:54`) | `internal/storage/recovery_tx_test.go`, `internal/storage/postgres_recovery_it_test.go`, `internal/scheduler/postgres_recovery_it_test.go` | `TestMemStoreRevokeRunnerLeasesAtomic`, `TestMemStoreRevokeRunnerLeasesCancelsWhenBudgetExhausted`, `TestMemStoreRecoveryCrashWindowRollsBack` (RevokeRunnerLeases), `TestPostgresIntegrationRevokeRunnerLeases`, `TestPostgresIntegrationRevokeRunnerLeasesCrashWindow`, `TestPostgresIntegrationRevokeRunnerLeasesTwoReplicas`, `TestPostgresIntegrationSchedulerKillSwitchTransactional`, `TestPostgresIntegrationSchedulerKillSwitchTwoReplicas` | requeue within budget (lease cleared, attempts unchanged, `job.runner_disabled_requeued` audit) or cancel when exhausted (`job.runner_disabled_cancelled`, dependents blocked); runner slots/counters and running→queued quota move exactly once; crash injection rolls back every staged write including audit (0 rows); two-replica race revokes the lease set exactly once |

| SEC-RECOVERY-003 | Queue-timeout expiry is ONE transaction: the queued/approval-waiting job cancels with the queue-timeout reason and its queued reservation, dependents, and run aggregate update together | Crash mid-expiry leaks a queued quota slot or double-releases it | `storage.ExpireQueuedJob` (`internal/storage/postgres_recovery.go:285`); `DBScheduler.RecoverExpired` (`internal/scheduler/scheduler.go:544`) | `internal/storage/recovery_tx_test.go`, `internal/storage/postgres_recovery_it_test.go`, `internal/scheduler/postgres_recovery_it_test.go` | `TestMemStoreExpireQueuedJobAtomic`, `TestMemStoreRecoveryCrashWindowRollsBack` (ExpireQueuedJob), `TestPostgresIntegrationExpireQueuedJob`, `TestPostgresIntegrationExpireQueuedJobCrashWindow`, `TestPostgresIntegrationExpireQueuedJobTwoReplicas`, `TestPostgresIntegrationSchedulerRecoverExpiredTransactional` | cancelled with `queue timeout`, queued reservation released once, dependent blocked; a deadline moved after the snapshot is left alone (expected-deadline guard); crash injection leaves zero partial writes; two-replica race expires exactly once |

| SEC-LEASE-017 | A failed fs/memory lease-claim snapshot rolls the claim back wholesale: job stays queued (attempts unconsumed, no token hash/runner id), runner slot free, run/deployment mirror restored; the `job.leased` audit and queue-latency observation run only after the durability check | Non-durable lease acknowledged → leaked runner slot, zombie attempt, or duplicate execution | `captureLeaseRollbackLocked`/`rollbackLeaseLocked` (`internal/server/server.go:2354-2419`) | `internal/server/fs_failure_matrix_test.go`, `internal/server/persist_durability_test.go` | `TestFSFailureMatrixEveryMutationFailsClosed` (job.lease case), `TestLeasePersistFailureWithholdsTokenAndRecovers` | 503 fixed body + `X-Kiwi-State: degraded` and no token; in-memory job/slot exactly pre-claim; heal re-leases the SAME job once (attempts +1, durable claim); the absent `job.leased` audit row is guaranteed by ordering, not directly asserted by these tests |
| SEC-OUTBOX-012 | Outbox flush releases EVERY claimed-but-unacked row before returning — ACKed, failed-ACK, and never-dispatched — instead of leaving them held until `OutboxClaimTTL` | Crash/error mid-flush strands rows under another replica's claim for the TTL | `Outbox.flushDB`/`flushDBBatch` `ReleaseOutboxClaim` on every non-ACK path (`internal/server/outbox.go:743-830`) | `internal/server/outbox_db_test.go` | `TestOutboxFlushDBReleasesUndispatchedClaimsOnAckError` | flush propagates the ack error after dispatching `a`,`b`; the claims for acked, failed, and undispatched rows are all released; a competing replica re-claims `b` and `c` in the same tick |

| SEC-OUTBOX-014 | Same outbox ID with different content is a typed invariant conflict (`ErrOutboxIDConflict`), never a silent success; identical content remains an idempotent success, including the durable-first replay after the local mirror is gone | Deterministic-key collision silently dropping or mutating an intent | `Outbox.Enqueue` + `sameOutboxContent` + `outboxIDConflictError` (`internal/server/outbox.go:254-313`) | `internal/server/outbox_test.go`, `internal/server/outbox_db_test.go` | `TestOutboxEnqueueSameIDConflictFS`, `TestOutboxEnqueueSameIDConflictDB` | same payload/kind succeeds; different payload or kind returns `ErrOutboxIDConflict` with the durable row count and local pending set unchanged (rows=1/pending=1); after mirror loss the same-content replay converges and different content fails |

| SEC-TLS-001 | A configured TLS listener actually serves TLS (certificates from `Server.TLSConfig`, runner client certs verified at the handshake); without `tls-cert` the same code path serves plaintext with no TLS config | Control plane advertised as TLS serving plaintext (credentials/leases in clear) | `internal/app/server_runner.go` (`h.TLSConfig` + `h.ServeTLS(ln, "", "")` vs `h.Serve(ln)`) | `internal/app/server_tls_socket_test.go` | `TestTLSSocketPlaintextRejectedAndHTTPSVerified`, `TestTLSSocketRunnerMTLSIdentity`, `TestTLSSocketNoTLSControlServesPlaintext` | plaintext probe refused on the TLS port; verified TLS ≥ 1.2 handshake succeeds; peer certificate identity reaches HTTP auth (cert-only register adopts CN, mismatch 403, cert-less runner 401); no-TLS control answers plaintext 200 and builds no TLS configuration |
| SEC-TIMEOUT-001 | Global `ReadTimeout`/`WriteTimeout` disabled for streaming routes, ordinary API routes re-bounded per request via `ResponseController`, bulk/SSE routes exempt; `ReadHeaderTimeout` 5s and `IdleTimeout` 90s kept | Global body deadline aborts legitimate 8 GiB uploads or SSE; or no bound at all on ordinary API bodies | `internal/app/server_runner.go` (`withAPIDeadlines`, `streamingRoute`; `ReadTimeout`/`WriteTimeout` 0, `ReadHeaderTimeout` 5s, `IdleTimeout` 90s) | `internal/app/server_tls_socket_test.go` | `TestTLSListenerTimeoutConfiguration`, `TestSlowUploadStreamingDeadlinePolicy`, `TestStreamingDeadlineStalledHeaderDropped` | server timeouts 0/0 with 5s/90s kept; multi-second chunked artifact upload (exempt route) succeeds while a stalled JSON body on the same server is cut; stalled header connection dropped or answered non-200, never served |
| SEC-LEAD-001 | Cached leadership is re-verified: every cached success pings the dedicated advisory-lock session, and a dead session drops the cache and runs a real acquisition instead of returning cached true | Dead leader keeps returning true while another replica holds the session lock (split brain) | `internal/storage/postgres.go` (`TryAcquireLeadership` + `leaderProbeTimeout`, `dropLeaderSessionLocked`; `ReleaseLeadership` clears the cache even on a failed unlock) | `internal/storage/postgres_leadership_stale_it_test.go` | `TestPostgresIntegrationLeadershipDeadSessionNoCachedTrue`, `TestPostgresIntegrationReleaseLeadershipClearsDeadSession` | session death inside the TTL: next call returns false (B's live lock respected) and leaves no cached conn/key/held-until; release over a dead session reports the failed round-trip AND clears the cache; reacquire after release succeeds |
| SEC-DRAIN-003 | Drain counts running jobs through one authoritative aggregate (`CountRunningJobs`); an unavailable count is UNKNOWN, never zero, and no `ListRuns` ceiling/per-run skip can influence it | DB drain concludes "zero active jobs" from a truncated or partially failing run/job scan | `storage.CountRunningJobs` (`internal/storage/postgres.go`, `internal/storage/faultstore.go` memStore/FaultyStore) + `Server.activeJobCount` (`internal/server/drain.go`) | `internal/storage/postgres_count_running_it_test.go`, `internal/storage/drain_count_test.go`, `internal/server/drain_test.go` | `TestPostgresIntegrationCountRunningJobs`, `TestMemStoreCountRunningJobs`, `TestFaultyStoreCountRunningJobs`, `TestDrainDBUnknownRunningCountKeepsWaiting`, `TestDrainDBAggregateCountIndependentOfRunScans`, `TestDrainMemoryModeCountKnown` | errored store count → drain keeps waiting (bounded) and `ActiveJobs()` never 0; aggregate still reports the running job with `ListRuns`/`ListJobsByRun` both broken and >10k runs; memory mode is authoritative and always known |
| SEC-DRAIN-004 | Drain durability is fail-closed: a `persistDrainFlagLocked` failure answers 503 + `X-Kiwi-State: degraded`, keeps the node draining in memory, and never claims a durable drain; the SIGTERM path drains memory-only | Acknowledged drain forgotten by restart (node resumes taking leases) | `internal/server/drain.go` (`persistDrainFlagLocked`, `beginDrain`, `drainServer`); signal path `BeginDrain` (`internal/app/server_runner.go:618`) | `internal/server/drain_test.go`, `internal/server/seam_json_test.go` | `TestDrainPersistFailureFailsClosed`, `TestSeamJSONDrainFlagPersistDropsFlag` | 503 with degraded body/header, no `drain.flag` on disk, still draining (readiness 503 + `X-Kiwi-Draining`), retry after recovery persists and survives restart; encoder seam drops the flag and returns the error instead of a false durable ack |
| SEC-OUTBOX-015 | Outbox claim releases run on a detached, bounded cleanup context (`context.WithoutCancel` + 5s `outboxClaimReleaseTimeout`); release failures are logged and the flush continues | A canceled flush leaves claimed rows invisible to every other replica until `OutboxClaimTTL` | `internal/server/outbox.go` (`releaseOutboxClaimCleanup`, `outboxClaimReleaseTimeout`) | `internal/server/outbox_db_test.go`, `internal/server/postgres_integration_test.go` | `TestOutboxFlushDBCancelledContextReleasesClaims`, `TestOutboxFlushDBReleaseFailureLoggedAndFlushContinues`, `TestPostgresIntegrationServerOutboxCancelledFlushReleasesClaims` | dispatch context canceled mid-batch: unACKed/undispatched rows are immediately reclaimable by another replica (never ACKed); a release error is logged, the row stays durable, and the flush proceeds; real-PG second pool re-claims b/c/d at once |
| SEC-PROV-004 | Artifact provenance identity is fail-closed: the authoritative run lookup (`GetRun` in DB mode, run map in memory) failing or missing aborts the upload with 503 before any staging; the raw error is never echoed | Provenance minted with an empty/forged identity, or bytes staged without a durable run record | `internal/server/blobs.go` (`uploadArtifact` authoritative run lookup before staging) | `internal/server/blobs_provenance_identity_test.go` | `TestArtifactUploadGetRunErrorFailsClosed`, `TestArtifactUploadRunNotFoundFailsClosed`, `TestArtifactUploadMemoryRunMissingFailsClosed`, `TestArtifactUploadProvenanceCarriesFullIdentity` | error/not-found/missing run → 503 "artifact provenance identity unavailable" with no raw store error, zero artifact rows, zero CAS objects and no staged files; happy path returns 201 with CAS-addressable signed provenance carrying repository/ref/commit |
| SEC-CTX-001 | Durable operations honor the caller's request context — `Outbox.Enqueue(ctx)`, versioned enqueue, enroll-grant auth/consume — while intentional detaches use `boundedDetach` (`context.WithoutCancel` + explicit timeout); a guard test pins the fs-owned write pinning | Canceled request performs a durable side effect, or a detached durable operation silently inherits cancellation and strands state | `internal/server/outbox.go` (`Enqueue(ctx)`, `enqueueVersionedLocked`, `boundedDetach`), `internal/server/enroll_grants.go` (`enrollGrantOK`, `consumeEnrollGrant`), `internal/server/runnerpki.go` (request ctx wiring) | `internal/server/outbox_context_test.go` | `TestOutboxEnqueueHonorsCanceledRequestContext`, `TestOutboxVersionedEnqueueCanceledKeepsExistingRow`, `TestOutboxFlushCanceledContextNeverAcks`, `TestEnrollGrantConsumeHonorsCanceledRequestContext`, `TestEnrollGrantOKRejectsCanceledRequest`, `TestBoundedDetachPersistsWhenOriginCanceled`, `TestBoundedDetachIsBounded`, `TestOwnedFilesPinContextCoupling` | canceled request: no enqueue/consume and the single-use grant stays consumable; versioned enqueue keeps the existing row; detached enqueue persists despite a canceled origin but its own deadline ends a stalled store; the owned-files guard fails on policy-exempt contexts |
| SEC-OPAQUE-001 | Every 5xx response body is opaque: `serverError`/`internalError` log request_id/method/path/status/error server-side and return only the fixed client message; a grep guard forbids raw `.Error()` text in 5xx bodies | Internal detail (filesystem paths, SQL, provider errors) disclosed to clients through 5xx bodies | `internal/server/server.go` (`serverError` :1053, `internalError` :1067) | `internal/server/internal_error_opaque_test.go` | `TestInternalErrorNoRaw5xxErrorBodies`, `TestInternalErrorStatusAndOpaqueBody`, `TestInternalErrorOpaqueBlobsList`, `TestInternalErrorOpaqueSchedulesList`, `TestInternalErrorOpaqueRunnerProfilesList`, `TestInternalErrorOpaqueOIDCConfiguration`, `TestInternalErrorOpaqueDeploymentRecord`, `TestInternalErrorOpaqueRunGet` | status preserved (500/502/503), sentinel error text never in the body (default "internal server error"), log record carries request id/method/path/status/detail; the package-wide grep guard scans non-test files and rejects any raw `.Error()` 5xx body; 131 handler call sites replaced (grep counts 132 calls including the `internalError`→`serverError` delegate) |

| SEC-OIDC-004 | A due OIDC rotation re-reads the shared ring under its OWN bounded context inside the fence and rotates only on a positively confirmed read; an unconfirmed refresh (expired fence context or unreachable store) serves the current signer without rotating | A replica that waited past its fence deadline rebuilds the ring from its stale signer, dropping the peer's freshly published active key from the ring and JWKS | `ensureOIDCSigner` + `refreshOIDCRingConfirmed` + `clusterKeyRotationFenceTimeout` (`internal/server/oidc.go`) | `internal/server/oidc_rotation_stale_refresh_test.go`, `internal/server/oidc_rotation_stale_refresh_it_test.go` | `TestOIDCRotationFenceStaleInFenceRefreshDoesNotOverwrite`, `TestIntegrationOIDCRotationFencePeerRotateWhileWaitingPostgres` | the peer's ring stays published (exactly one write); the waiting replica serves the peer's key and its issued token verifies under it; no rotation from an unconfirmed rebuild |

| SEC-EXEC-018 | The runner-local cache tree is a SINGLE shared capacity domain: one cache.Manager per runner owns the root + policy and serializes reservations, eviction and explicit passes across the per-job Stores (each job still builds its own Store, but all reference the same manager). Saves AND remote restores reserve the worst-case (exact Content-Length when advertised) BEFORE writing bytes; the manager evicts cold entries oldest-first until retained+inflight <= MaxBytes and entries+inflight <= MaxEntries, measures retained bytes from the directory itself (no drift), and refuses an operation that can never fit with ErrCacheBudgetExceeded. Publication runs under the same manager lock and retires the worst-case charge at the instant the bytes become visible, so a concurrent reservation can never double-count a just-published entry and evict it (the race the deterministic Publish test pins). Reservation states are explicit (open/published/released/retained): Release only returns an OPEN charge, so the deferred Release after a successful Publish can never drive inflight negative and oversubscribe the budget, and an aborted operation whose temp file cannot be removed retains its charge as cleanup debt retried by RetryTempCleanup. Each runner process owns a private <cache root>/<runner instance id> namespace because the manager's ledger and locks are process-local: two runner processes sharing a root can no longer double-spend one physical budget, and Run replaces a manager whose root or policy changed while direct callers keep lazy construction. Namespace ownership is a real cross-process primitive: NewManager takes an exclusive flock on <namespace>/kiwi-cache.lock BEFORE any destructive startup reclamation, so a second live process with the same runner identity (duplicate registration is allowed by the control plane) is refused with ErrCacheDirOwned instead of deleting the first process's live temp files; every temp/reclaim/write is provably against a dead owner only under that lock, Close releases it after a final retry, and closed managers refuse Reserve/Publish/Retain/Reclaim. Startup ownership is transactional: Run acquires cache ownership, and if the staging configuration that follows fails, a rollback releases the cache namespace; a fully joined stop closes it alongside staging. Crash consistency is rebuilt from physical truth at startup: NewManager reclaims abandoned Kiwi temp shapes (.<key>.tar.gz-*.tmp / .<key>.remote-*.tmp, never following symlinks or directories) and charges the ones it cannot remove, so repeated abnormal restarts cannot strand unaccounted multi-GiB temps outside the budget; the pre-namespace shared layout (<root>/cache/*.tar.gz) is reclaimed once per root because the cache is disposable and the namespaced layout is the only supported one. Manager replacement first retries temp debt and REFUSES a changed root/policy while any charge remains (only that manager accounts for the bytes), and Run configures the cache manager BEFORE the staging budget so a cache refusal returns before the externally owned staging lock/process-registry entry is acquired (a Run that never started must not leak staging ownership). Incomplete legacy-layout reclamation is retried on every cache maintenance pass, not only at manager installation, so a transient EBUSY during an upgrade cannot strand the old shared tree for the process lifetime. Publish fails closed with ErrInvalidReservation BEFORE running the callback, so a foreign/ended/nil reservation can never create physical bytes outside the ledger; RetryTempCleanup locks before inspecting pendingTemps (the unlocked len was a real data race). Eviction re-stats each entry (freshness fence) so a concurrently refreshed key is skipped, not deleted. The per-archive bound is one resolved value (`MaxStoredBytes`) driving the local cap, restore verification and transport preflight. Defaults 32 GiB / 4096 / 14 days, flags `--cache-max-bytes`/`--cache-max-entries`/`--cache-max-age` | A pipeline rotating its logical cache key filling the runner host's disk forever; a sequence of remote restores (up to 16 per job) bypassing the aggregate bound between maintenance passes because only saves pruned and each job held an independent mutex; inflight downloads not counted against the budget; or a stale ranking deleting an entry a concurrent save just refreshed | `internal/cache/manager.go` (`Manager`, `Reserve`, `Reservation`, `ErrCacheBudgetExceeded`), `internal/cache/cache.go` (Store.Manager, save/remote-restore reservations, `MaxStoredBytes`), `internal/cache/retention.go` (fenced eviction, `Prune` delegation), `internal/runner/cache.go` (`cacheManager`, `newJobCache`, `pruneJobCache`), `internal/runner/runner.go` (cache maintenance cadence, `Config.Cache*`), `internal/app/server_runner.go` (cache flags) | `internal/cache/manager_test.go`, `internal/cache/retention_test.go`, `internal/runner/cache_retention_test.go`, `internal/runner/maintenance_test.go` | `TestManagerReserveEvictsColdEntries`, `TestManagerPublishIsAtomicWithVisibility`, `TestManagerPublishFailureKeepsReservation`, `TestManagerRetryTempCleanupConcurrentRetain`, `TestManagerRestartReclaimsAbandonedSaveTemp`, `TestManagerRestartReclaimsAbandonedRestoreTemp`, `TestManagerRestartAccountsUndeletableAbandonedTemp`, `TestCrashLeftTempCannotBypassMaxBytes`, `TestReclaimLegacyLayout`, `TestNamespaceLockRejectsSecondManager`, `TestCacheNamespaceSecondProcessCannotReclaimLiveTemp` (true second process; mutation-checked), `TestAbandonedTempReclaimedAfterPriorProcessExits`, `TestManagerClosedRefusesOperations`, `TestCacheNamespaceOwnershipReleasedOnStartupFailure`, `TestCacheNamespaceOwnershipReleasedOnCleanShutdown`, `TestRunnerStartupReclaimsLegacySharedCache`, `TestRunnerCacheManagerReplacementRefusedWithDebt`, `TestCacheManagerStartupFailureDoesNotRetainStagingOwnership`, `TestRunnerPruneJobCacheRetriesLegacyReclaim`, `TestPublishedReservationDeferredReleaseIsNoop`, `TestPublishedReservationDoesNotDriveInflightNegative`, `TestPublishedReservationDoesNotDriveEntryCountNegative`, `TestRepeatedSuccessfulSavesPreserveAggregateBound`, `TestRepeatedSuccessfulRestoresPreserveAggregateBound`, `TestSaveContextTempCleanupFailureKeepsCharge`, `TestFetchRemoteTempCleanupFailureKeepsCharge`, `TestPruneFailedAgeEvictionStaysAccounted`, `TestRunnerCacheNamespacesAreProcessPrivate`, `TestRunnerCacheManagerFollowsConfigAcrossRestarts`, `TestManagerReserveRejectsOversize`, `TestManagerReserveHonorsInflightAndRelease`, `TestManagerReserveContextCanceled`, `TestManagerPruneSerializesWithReservations`, `TestCacheAggregateBoundAcrossStores`, `TestCacheMaxBytesNeverExceededPhysically`, `TestRemoteRestoreEvictsBeforeExceedingLimit`, `TestCacheInflightReservationReleasedOnCancel`, `TestLocalPruneDoesNotDeleteFreshConcurrentSave`, `TestRunnerCacheAggregateBoundAcrossJobs`, `TestRunnerCacheAggregateBoundIncludesConcurrentRestores`, `TestRunnerCacheRetentionEvictsToBounds`, `TestRunnerCachePreflightUsesResolvedBound`, `TestPruneEnforcesByteAndEntryBounds`, `TestPruneEvictsAgedEntries`, `TestPruneRemovalFailureKeepsAccountingAndRetries` | mutation-reproduced: without the shared manager concurrent jobs/stores exceed the 2500-byte bound and remote restores never evict before writing; | SEC-EXEC-020 | Durable shared-cache manifests have retention and admission bounds: `storage.CacheManifestPruner.PruneCacheManifests` ranks manifests per (repository, TRUST DOMAIN) namespace (newest first, deterministic tiebreak) and evicts by age, per-namespace count and per-namespace LOGICAL referenced bytes (sum of manifest sizes, not digest-deduplicated; documented as a conservative upper bound) in one SQL statement (Postgres) or the mirrored in-memory ranking; the victim identity carries created_at + blob_sha256 and the DELETE re-matches on them, so a concurrently refreshed row is never deleted on a stale ranking. | SEC-LEASE-010 | DB-mode lease lifetime is wholly database-clock authoritative: a claim carrying `LeaseClaim.TTL` stores `clock_timestamp() + TTL` inside the claim transaction and returns the stored instant (the caller's absolute ExpiresAt is ignored by the DB-clock path); heartbeat renewal writes `clock_timestamp() + TTL` ONLY while the stored lease is still live at that clock (`lease_expires_at > clock_timestamp()`), so a delayed heartbeat cannot resurrect a recoverable lease; recovery discovery (`ListExpiredRunningJobs`/`ListQueueTimedOutJobs`) and application (`RecoverExpiredLease`/`ExpireQueuedJob`) compare against a post-lock `clock_timestamp()`; handler expiry checks are documented ADVISORY with the store as authority. | SEC-AMB-001 | An external create command error is never proof the resource was not created: `docker run` (job and service containers), `docker network create` and `tart clone` all attempt a bounded remove-by-name after ANY command error, and only a proven absence (`rm` exit 0 or a positive not-found) may tear down the workspace ownership/quota protections. Anything else retains the resource identity and reports `CleanupDebt`, so the runner keeps the ledger and stops leasing; the service container name joins the owned cleanup set BEFORE its `docker run` (not after success), and every `cleanupAll` error is joined and reported instead of discarded. A failed `StartJob` always runs the backend's retryable `CloseJob`, whose unproven removal is reported as debt | A daemon creates the container/network/clone, then the client response/socket stalls and the bounded command context expires: previously Kiwi concluded "start failed", restored workspace ownership/quota and deleted the workspace while a live resource still referenced it; a service container that started before the client timed out was absent from the cleanup set; a discarded partial-start cleanup error left an unproven resource | `internal/executor/container.go` (`dockerCleanupCommand`, `isContainerAbsentError`, `StartJob`, `CloseJob`), `internal/executor/services.go` (`isNetworkAbsentError`, owned-set-before-run, joined cleanup errors), `internal/executor/tart.go` (`CloseJob`/`deleteCloneBounded` debt reports), `internal/executor/executor.go` (`runJob` StartJob-failure CloseJob path, `CleanupNetwork`/`CleanupVM`) | `internal/executor/ambiguous_create_test.go`, `internal/executor/setup_phase_test.go`, `internal/executor/backend_fake_cov_test.go` | `TestDockerRunAmbiguousCreateRemovesThenSucceeds`, `TestDockerRunAmbiguousCreateCleanupFailureReportsDebt`, `TestServiceNetworkAmbiguousCreateReportsDebt`, `TestServiceContainerAmbiguousCreateIsInOwnedSet`, `TestStartJobFailureInvokesCloseJobAndReportsDebt`, `TestTartCloneFailureCleanupFailureReportsDebt`, `TestTartRunStartCleanupFailureReportsDebt`, `TestContainerBackendStartJobHangingRunBounded`, `TestStartContainerServicesHangingNetworkCreateBounded` | mutation-reproduced: removing the post-ambiguity remove-by-name leaves the fake's container state and the "container may exist" error unproduced; appending the service name only after success removes it from the rm log; discarding `CloseJob`'s error loses the debt report; a hanging daemon still returns within the shrunk cleanup/setup ceilings |
| SEC-IDEM-001 | Run submission carries a DURABLE client-operation identity: an optional `Idempotency-Key` header on `POST /api/v1/runs` and the rerun routes is bound to the canonical digest of the client-visible request (kind + source run + repo/ref/sha/pipeline/changed files/metadata) and persisted as a `(canonical repo, key) -> (digest, run)` receipt in the SAME transaction/snapshot write as the run. Same key + same digest returns the original run before pipeline resolution or admission; same key + different digest fails closed with 409 `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` and enqueues nothing; a receipt whose run was pruned is a 409 rather than a silent second execution. Receipts age out through the same GC (TTL, bounded DB delete batches), and `kiwi dispatch`/`kiwi rerun` generate ONE key per invocation and reuse it across bounded transport/429/5xx retries | A client POST commits, the proxy connection dies before the 202 arrives, and the identical retry (or a replica racing the first submission) enqueues a second run with a new random ID: duplicate builds, artifact publication, deployments and downstream generated work | `internal/server/idempotency.go`, `internal/server/server.go` (`submit`, `rerunRun`, `rerunRunDB`, `enqueueID` pre-check, `enqueueDB` in-transaction claim, fs receipt in the same snapshot, GC prune), `internal/server/types.go`, `internal/storage/store.go` (`RunIdempotencyClaim`, `RunIdempotencyStore`, sentinels), `internal/storage/postgres.go` (`claimRunIdempotencyTx`, `FindRunIdempotency`, `PruneRunIdempotency`), `internal/storage/faultstore.go`, `internal/storage/migrations/0037_run_idempotency.sql`, `internal/app/dispatch.go`, `internal/app/ops.go` | `internal/server/idempotency_test.go`, `internal/storage/run_idempotency_test.go` | `TestSubmitIdempotencyReplayFSAndDB`, `TestSubmitIdempotencyReplaySurvivesRestartFS`, `TestSubmitIdempotencyRejectsInvalidKey`, `TestSubmitIdempotencyConcurrentFirstSubmissionDB`, `TestRerunIdempotencyReplayFS`, `TestIdempotencyReceiptRetentionFS`, `TestIdempotencyReceiptPruneDB`, `TestMemStoreRunIdempotencyClaim`, `TestFaultyStoreRunIdempotencyPassThrough`, `TestPostgresIntegrationRunIdempotencyClaimAtomicity`, `TestPostgresIntegrationRunIdempotencyConcurrentRace` | mutation-reproduced: removing the receipt makes the replay/restart/race tests enqueue a second run; skipping the in-transaction claim lets concurrent first submissions both insert; storing it outside the snapshot loses the post-restart replay; a changed sha/pipeline under the same key is 409 with exactly one run row |
| SEC-SCHED-001 | One `/next` lease request never materializes the global queue: the scheduler fetches candidates through `storage.QueuedJobPageStore` in keyset pages of at most `candidate_page_size`, scans at most `max_candidate_rows` per request (both configurable, defaults 256/4096), follows the cursor across pages while nothing is claimable, and keeps the historical whole-queue fallback for stores without the contract. The SQL page expresses the SAME aged priority as Go (`priority + floor((now-created_at)/600s)`, clamped at zero) and pushes definitely-expired persisted queue deadlines into the query. The queue-reason explainer uses the same bounded fetch. | SEC-SCHED-002 | Aged large jobs cannot be starved by backfill: the first resource-blocked but otherwise eligible candidate in aged order becomes the request-local RESERVATION HEAD (only when the runner's configured capacity could ever fit it, after `reservation_wait`). | SEC-SECRET-003 (R13) | fs one-time secret receipts are compacted by LIFECYCLE: a receipt whose `(job, generation)` is non-terminal and whose lease is unexpired is retained regardless of the 16,384 cap (overshoot documented and bounded by active leases); only terminal/absent/older-generation receipts are evicted oldest-first, and tombstones coalesce so a released key can never resurrect. One-time delivery is RETRY-STABLE: the sealed envelope is persisted in the claim (migration 0044) bound to the runner's ephemeral public key; an identical retry (same job/generation/secret/public) returns the EXACT same ciphertext with no second claim or audit, a different public key stays 409, and a stale generation is refused by lease validation before any replay. The runner caches its ephemeral keypair per `(job,generation,secret)` so a transport retry reuses the same key | recency-only compaction could evict a live claim after 16,384 unrelated deliveries and re-deliver the secret to the same live generation; and a commit whose TCP response was lost made every retry 409 forever, turning normal response loss into an unrecoverable job failure | `internal/server/secret.go` (`secretReceipts` envelope map, `secretReceiptLiveLocked`, compaction, `deliverSecret` replay), `internal/storage/migrations/0044_secret_delivery_replay.sql`, `internal/storage/postgres_secret_issue.go` (`CommitSecretIssuance` replay result + `LookupSecretIssuance`), `internal/storage/store.go` (`SealedSecretDelivery`, `ReplayableSecretIssuance`), `internal/secretbroker/remote.go` (ephemeral key cache) | `internal/server/secret_replay_test.go`, `internal/server/secret_receipts_edges2_test.go`, `internal/storage/postgres_secret_lease_commit_it_test.go`, `internal/secretbroker/remote_test.go` | `TestSecretRetrySameKeyReplaysMemory`, `TestSecretRetrySameKeyReplaysAfterJournalRestart` (the live receipt is the OLDEST journal record under 16,384 dead ones, so recency-only trimming would evict exactly it; lifecycle retention keeps it and the restart replays with a failing broker — mutation-verified), `TestSecretRetryDifferentKeyConflictsMemory`, `TestSecretDBFakeSameKeyRetryReplays`, PG `TestIntegrationSecretIssuanceReplayAfterLostResponse` (identical envelope, one claim/audit; different key 409; stale generation refused pre-replay), runner/secretbroker transport-drop key-reuse test | mutation-reproduced: recency-only compaction drops the live key and the same generation re-obtains the secret; returning 409 on identical retries fails the replay tests; minting a fresh envelope on replay yields a different ciphertext |
| SEC-EVENTS-001 (R13) | Kiwi owns a canonical durable execution event stream: `execution_events` (BIGSERIAL cursor, schema_version, run/job, attempt=lease generation, event_type, from/to status, payload, clock timestamp) with indexes by `(run_id, seq)` and `(job_id, seq)`. PostgreSQL appends via `AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OF status` triggers on jobs/runs inside the SAME transaction as the state change (rollback removes events; `IS DISTINCT FROM` suppresses no-ops), so no transition can be missed without failing the mutation. | SEC-EVENTS-002 (R14) | The public execution-event cursor is COMMIT-ORDERED: migration 0046 replaces the BIGSERIAL default with a single-row transactional cursor (`UPDATE execution_event_cursor SET value=value+1 RETURNING value` inside the same transaction as the event insert), so a later event transaction blocks on the row lock until the earlier one commits/rolls back; allocation order equals visible order and a rolled-back allocation is not burned.
